Sawal: Actinide (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Actinium, Thorium, Protactinium, Uranium, Neptunium, Plutonium, Americium, Curium, Berkelium, Californium, Einsteinium, Fermium, Mendelevium, Nobelium, Lawrencium.
Sawal: Alkali metal (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Lithium, Sodium, Potassium, Rubidium, Caesium, Francium.
Sawal: Alkaline earth metal (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Beryllium, Magnesium, Calcium, Strontium, Barium, Radium.
Sawal: Halogen (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Fluorine, Chlorine, Bromine, Iodine, Astatine.
Sawal: Lanthanide (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Lanthanum, Cerium, Praseodymium, Neodymium, Promethium, Samarium, Europium, Gadolinium, Terbium, Dysprosium, Holmium, Erbium, Thulium, Ytterbium, Lutetium.
Sawal: Metalloid (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Boron, Silicon, Germanium, Arsenic, Antimony, Tellurium, Polonium.
Sawal: Noble gas (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Helium, Neon, Argon, Krypton, Xenon, Radon.
Sawal: Nonmetal (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Hydrogen, Carbon, Nitrogen, Oxygen, Phosphorus, Sulfur, Selenium.
Sawal: Post-transition metal (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Aluminium, Gallium, Indium, Tin, Thallium, Lead, Bismuth.
Sawal: Transition metal (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Scandium, Titanium, Vanadium, Chromium, Manganese, Iron, Cobalt, Nickel, Copper, Zinc, Yttrium, Zirconium, Niobium, Molybdenum, Technetium, Ruthenium, Rhodium, Palladium, Silver, Cadmium, Hafnium, Tantalum, Tungsten, Rhenium, Osmium, Iridium, Platinum, Gold, Mercury, Rutherfordium, Dubnium, Seaborgium, Bohrium, Hassium.
Sawal: Unknown (elements) kaunse hain?
Jawab: Meitnerium, Darmstadtium, Roentgenium, Copernicium, Nihonium, Flerovium, Moscovium, Livermorium, Tennessine, Oganesson.

Jawab: Ar-Rasheed.
Sawal: Asma-ul-Husna: As-Sabur ka ma'ni kya hai?
Jawab: The Most Patient - Allah ke 99 naam me se ek.
Sawal: Allah ke 99 naam me 'The Most Patient' wala kaunsa naam hai?
Jawab: As-Sabur.
Sawal: Sooraj (Sun) kya hai?
Jawab: Sooraj ek sitara (star) hai - diameter ~1.39 million km, zameen se taqreeban 150 million km door.
Sawal: Sooraj ki umar kitni hai?
Jawab: Taqreeban 4.6 billion saal.
Sawal: Chand se zameen ka faasla?
Jawab: Taqreeban 384,400 km.
Sawal: Zameen ka qutr (diameter) kitna hai?
Jawab: Taqreeban 12,742 km.
Sawal: Zameen Sooraj ke gird ek chakkar me kitna waqt leti hai?
Jawab: 365.25 din (1 saal).
Sawal: Zameen apne mehwar par kitni der me ghumti hai?
Jawab: 24 ghante (1 din).
Sawal: Sab se bara grah kaunsa hai?
Jawab: Jupiter (Mushtari) - zameen se ~11 guna bara qutr.
Sawal: Sab se chhota grah?
Jawab: Mercury (Atarad) - Pluto ko 2006 me dwarf-grah bana diya gaya.
Sawal: Saur-mandal me kitne grah hain?
Jawab: 8 (Mercury, Venus, Earth, Mars, Jupiter, Saturn, Uranus, Neptune).
Sawal: Sooraj se sab se door grah?
Jawab: Neptune (Qibti).
Sawal: Garm-tareen grah?
Jawab: Venus (Zuhra) - surface ~465C (greenhouse-gas wajah se).
Sawal: Surkh (red) grah?
Jawab: Mars (Mirrikh) - surkh mitti (iron-oxide) ki wajah se.
Sawal: Mars ke chand?
Jawab: Do - Phobos aur Deimos.
Sawal: Jupiter ka mashhoor toofan?
Jawab: Great Red Spot - sadiyon se chala aane wala bara toofan.
Sawal: Saturn ke rings kis cheez ke hain?
Jawab: Barf aur chattan ke tukray (ice/rock particles).
Sawal: Chand par pehla insaan kaun gaya?
Jawab: Neil Armstrong - 20 July 1969 (Apollo 11).
Sawal: Chand par kitne insaan chale?
Jawab: Sirf 12 (Apollo missions 1969-1972).
Sawal: Chand ki gravity zameen se kitne guna kam?
Jawab: Taqreeban 1/6 (chhota wazan wahan 6 guna halka mehsoos hota hai).
Sawal: Sab se bada chand (moon) kis grah ka?
Jawab: Jupiter ka Ganymede - solar system ka sab se bara moon.
Sawal: Titaa kiska chand hai?
Jawab: Saturn - apni ghani rings aur atmosphere ke liye mashhoor.
Sawal: Black hole kya hai?
Jawab: Aisi jism jiska gravity itna zor-dar hai ke roshni bhi nikal na sake.
Sawal: Aasmaan me sitare kitne door hote hain?
Jawab: Light-years me - 1 light-year ~9.46 trillion km.
Sawal: Sab se qareeb ka sitara?
Jawab: Proxima Centauri - ~4.2 light-years door.
Sawal: Milky Way kya hai?
Jawab: Hamari galaxy - taqreeban 100-400 billion sitare.
Sawal: Shooting star kya hoti hai?
Jawab: Asal me chhota meteor jo hawa me jal jata hai.
Sawal: Comet (dekhta-tara) kya hai?
Jawab: Barf + dhool ka gumbad jiska poonchh Sooraj ki garmi se banta hai.
Sawal: Halley's comet kab wapas aata hai?
Jawab: Har ~76 saal me (agla 2061 me).
Sawal: ISS kya hai?
Jawab: International Space Station - zameen se ~400 km upar, 90 min me zameen ka chakkar.
Sawal: Pehla insaan space me kaun?
Jawab: Yuri Gagarin (Soviet) - 12 April 1961.
Sawal: Pehla aurat space me?
Jawab: Valentina Tereshkova - 1963.
Sawal: NASA ka full form?
Jawab: National Aeronautics and Space Administration (1958 me bana).
Sawal: Roshni ki raftaar?
Jawab: Vacuum me taqreeban 299,792 km/s (~300,000 km/s).
Sawal: Awaaz ki raftaar hawa me?
Jawab: Taqreeban 343 m/s (20C par).
Sawal: Sooraj ki roshni zameen tak kitne minute me?
Jawab: Taqreeban 8 minute 20 second.
Sawal: Gravity ki raftaar (acceleration)?
Jawab: Zameen par ~9.8 m/s^2.
Sawal: Absolute zero kitna?
Jawab: -273.15C (0 Kelvin) - isse thanda mumkin nahi.
Sawal: Paani ka boiling point?
Jawab: 100C (sea-level par); freezing 0C.
Sawal: Newton ka pehla qanoon kya hai?
Jawab: Jism harakat-halat me rehta hai jab tak bahari force na lage (inertia).
Sawal: Newton ka doosra qanoon?
Jawab: Force = mass  x  acceleration (F = ma).
Sawal: Newton ka teesra qanoon?
Jawab: Har action ka barabar-muqabla reaction hota hai.
Sawal: E = mc^2 kis ka formula hai?
Jawab: Albert Einstein (mass-energy equivalence).
Sawal: Rainbow me kitne rang?
Jawab: 7 - surkh, naranji, zard, sabz, aasmani, neela, banafsji.
Sawal: Rainbow kab banti hai?
Jawab: Jab sooraj ki roshni boondon se takrant hai (reflection + refraction).
Sawal: Baa-dast-o-baar (lightning) ka temperature?
Jawab: Taqreeban 30,000C - sooraj ki sath se bhi garam.
Sawal: Bijli kaise banti hai?
Jawab: Barish ke baadal me charge-alag-honi se (positive/negative separation).
Sawal: Awaaz space me sunai deti hai?
Jawab: Nahi - space me hawa (medium) nahi, isliye awaaz na phailti hai.
Sawal: Magnet ke konse qutub?
Jawab: Do - North aur South; ek-sa jaise qutub izzat-rukh (repel) karte hain.
Sawal: Electricity ki lazzat (current) kya hai?
Jawab: Electrons ka bahao (flow).
Sawal: Ohm ka qanoon?
Jawab: Voltage = Current  x  Resistance (V = I  x  R).
Sawal: Pakistan ka standard voltage?
Jawab: 220-240V AC, frequency 50 Hz.
Sawal: Laser ka full form?
Jawab: Light Amplification by Stimulated Emission of Radiation.
Sawal: Nuclear fission kya hai?
Jawab: Bhaari atom-core (uranium) ka do tukron me toot-kar energy dena.
Sawal: Sooraj ki energy ka sanchot?
Jawab: Nuclear fusion - hydrogen ke nucleus mil-kar helium bante hain.
Sawal: Uranium-238 ka half-life?
Jawab: Taqreeban 4.5 billion saal.
Sawal: X-rays ka izhar (discovery) kisne kiya?
Jawab: Wilhelm Rntgen - 1895.
Sawal: Radioactivity ka izhar kisne kiya?
Jawab: Henri Becquerel (1896); aur Marie Curie ne aage barhaya.
Sawal: Telescopes se sab se door ka object?
Jawab: Light-years - billions door ki galaxies (JWST aur Hubble se).
Sawal: Hubble telescope kab launch hua?
Jawab: 1990.
Sawal: Sooraj ki chamak (brightness) ka sanchot?
Jawab: Har second ~400 million ton hydrogen fusion me tabdeel hota hai.
Sawal: Hawa me oxygen kitna percent?
Jawab: Taqreeban 21%; nitrogen ~78%.
Sawal: Sea-level par hawa ka dabao?
Jawab: Taqreeban 101.3 kPa (1 atmosphere).
Sawal: Sab se garm record temperature?
Jawab: 56.7C - Death Valley, USA (1913).
Sawal: Sab se thanda record temperature?
Jawab: -89.2C - Vostok Station, Antarctica (1983).
Sawal: Zameen ka sab se ooncha nuqta?
Jawab: Mount Everest - 8,849 m.
Sawal: Zameen ka sab se nichla nuqta (land)?
Jawab: Dead Sea shore - taqreeban -430 m.
Sawal: Sab se gehra samandari nuqta?
Jawab: Mariana Trench - Challenger Deep ~10,935 m.
Sawal: Sab se lamba darya?
Jawab: Nile (taqreeban 6,650 km) - volume me Amazon sab se bara.
Sawal: Sab se bara samandar (ocean)?
Jawab: Pacific - taqreeban 165 million km^2.
Sawal: Sab se bara registan (hot desert)?
Jawab: Sahara - taqreeban 9.2 million km^2.
Sawal: Sab se bara jazeera (island)?
Jawab: Greenland - taqreeban 2.16 million km^2.
Sawal: Sab se gehra jheel?
Jawab: Lake Baikal (Russia) - 1,642 m.
Sawal: Sab se bara jheel?
Jawab: Caspian Sea - taqreeban 371,000 km^2.
Sawal: Sab se ooncha jharna (waterfall)?
Jawab: Angel Falls (Venezuela) - 979 m.
Sawal: Zameen par paani kitna percent?
Jawab: ~71% satah; freshwater sirf ~2.5%.
Sawal: Samandar ka namak-kitna?
Jawab: Taqreeban 3.5% (average salinity).
Sawal: Antarctica kaisi jagah hai?
Jawab: Sab se thandi, sab se zyada barf-wali (~98% ice), sab se kam barish wali.
Sawal: Sab se lamba pahari-silsila?
Jawab: Andes (South America) - taqreeban 7,000 km.
Sawal: Himalaya ka ma'ni?
Jawab: "Barf ka ghar" (Sanskrit: him = barf, alaya = ghar).
Sawal: K2 ki bulandi?
Jawab: 8,611 m - duniya ka doosra ooncha pahar, Pakistan (Karakoram) me.
Sawal: Nanga Parbat ka laqab?
Jawab: "Killer Mountain" - 8,126 m.
Sawal: Suez Canal kab khula?
Jawab: 1869 - Egypt me, Europe-Asia ka samandari rasta chhota karta hai.
Sawal: Panama Canal kab khula?
Jawab: 1914.
Sawal: Zameen ke kitne qitte (continents)?
Jawab: 7 - Asia, Africa, North America, South America, Antarctica, Europe, Australia.
Sawal: Zameen ke kitne samandar (oceans)?
Jawab: 5 - Pacific, Atlantic, Indian, Southern, Arctic.
Sawal: Sab se bara qit'a (continent)?
Jawab: Asia - taqreeban 44 million km^2.
Sawal: Sab se chhota qit'a?
Jawab: Australia (Oceania).
Sawal: Zameen ki population kitni hai?
Jawab: 8 billion se zyada (2022 ke baad).
Sawal: Equator kya hai?
Jawab: Zameen ka 0 latitude - zameen ko do nisf me qatata hai.
Sawal: Greenwich kya hai?
Jawab: London ki jagah jahan se 0 longitude (Prime Meridian) ginta hai.
Sawal: Zameen par kitne time-zones?
Jawab: 24 main zones (UTC par mabni).
Sawal: Mausam ke mousam (seasons) kyun badalte hain?
Jawab: Zameen ke mehwar ka 23.5 jhukao (tilt) aur Sooraj ke gird chakkar.
Sawal: Sab se bara pahaari-silsila pani ke neeche?
Jawab: Mid-Oceanic Ridge - ~65,000 km lamba.
Sawal: Din-rat kyun hote hain?
Jawab: Zameen ki apne-mehwar par ghoomna (rotation).
Sawal: Sab se bada aashiyana-dar (bay)?
Jawab: Bay of Bengal.
Sawal: Great Barrier Reef kahan hai?
Jawab: Australia - duniya ka sab se bara coral reef (~344,000 km^2).
Sawal: Amazon jangal kahan hai?
Jawab: South America - ~5.5 million km^2, duniya ke ~10% janwar-patte idhar hain.
Sawal: Sahara ka ma'ni?
Jawab: Arabic "sahra" - registan.
Sawal: Zameen ki sab se ghani (dense) tabqa?
Jawab: Core (iron-nickel) - andar-inner core ~5,700C garam.
Sawal: Volcano kya hai?
Jawab: Zameen ke andar ka pighla-pathar (magma) jo satah par nikal aaye.
Sawal: Earthquake (zameen-janji) kaise aati hai?
Jawab: Zameen ki plates (tectonic) ke hilne jhatke se.
Sawal: Richter scale kya napta hai?
Jawab: Earthquake ki taaqat (magnitude).
Sawal: Pehla insani civilization jise hum jante hain?
Jawab: Mesopotamia (Tigris-Euphrates) - ~3500 BC.
Sawal: Indus Valley civilization kahan thi?
Jawab: Sindh-Punjab (Pakistan) - Mohenjo-daro, Harappa ~2500 BC.
Sawal: Mohenjo-daro ka ma'ni?
Jawab: "Murdoun ka tilla" (Sindhi).
Sawal: Pakistan ka sab se bara shehar?
Jawab: Karachi.
Sawal: Pakistan ki raajdhaani?
Jawab: Islamabad (1960s me Karachi se shift hui).
Sawal: Pakistan ke kitne subay (provinces)?
Jawab: 4 - Punjab, Sindh, KP, Balochistan (+Islamabad Capital Territory, GB, AJK).
Sawal: Pakistan ka sab se lamba darya?
Jawab: Sindh (Indus) - taqreeban 3,180 km.
Sawal: Tarbela Dam kis darya par hai?
Jawab: Sindh (Indus) - duniya ka sab se bara earth-filled dam.
Sawal: Pakistan ki nuclear tests kab?
Jawab: 28 May 1998 - Chagai, Balochistan.
Sawal: Pakistan ka quaid?
Jawab: Quaid-e-Azam Muhammad Ali Jinnah (1876-1948).
Sawal: Pakistan kab bana?
Jawab: 14 August 1947.
Sawal: Lahore Resolution kab hua?
Jawab: 23 March 1940.
Sawal: Pakistan ke pehle Governor-General?
Jawab: Quaid-e-Azam Muhammad Ali Jinnah.
Sawal: Pakistan ke pehle Wazir-e-Azam?
Jawab: Liaquat Ali Khan.
Sawal: Pakistan ka national janwar?
Jawab: Markhor.
Sawal: Pakistan ka national parinda?
Jawab: Chukar (partridge).
Sawal: Pakistan ka national phool?
Jawab: Jasmine (chambeli).
Sawal: Pakistan ka national phal?
Jawab: Mango (aam).
Sawal: Allama Iqbal kab peda hue?
Jawab: 9 November 1877 - Sialkot.
Sawal: Quaid-e-Azam kab peda hue?
Jawab: 25 December 1876 - Karachi.
Sawal: Dil (heart) ek din me kitni dafa dharkta hai?
Jawab: Taqreeban 100,000 dafa.
Sawal: Dil ek minute me kitna khoon pump karta hai?
Jawab: Taqreeban 5 litre (aarum se).
Sawal: Insani dimagh me kitne neurons?
Jawab: Taqreeban 86 billion.
Sawal: Insan ke kitni haddiyan (bones)?
Jawab: Bara-ho-kar 206 (baccha ~270 se shuru).
Sawal: Insan ke kitne muscles?
Jawab: Taqreeban 600 se zyada.
Sawal: Jism me kitna khoon?
Jawab: ~5-6 litre (baligh insan).
Sawal: Red blood cell ki zindagi?
Jawab: Taqreeban 120 din.
Sawal: DNA me kitne base-pairs?
Jawab: Taqreeban 3 billion.
Sawal: Jism ka sab se bara organ?
Jawab: Khaal (skin) - ~2 m^2.
Sawal: Khoon ka rang laal kyun?
Jawab: Hemoglobin me iron (loha) hota hai.
Sawal: Stomach ka acid kitna tez?
Jawab: pH 1.5-3.5 (food kharab karne ke liye kafi tez).
Sawal: Phephron (lungs) me kitne alveoli?
Jawab: 300-500 million.
Sawal: Normal jism ka darja-hararat?
Jawab: Taqreeban 37C (98.6F).
Sawal: Normal blood pressure?
Jawab: ~120/80 mmHg.
Sawal: Insaan ki aankh kitne rang pehchanti hai?
Jawab: ~10 million shades tak.
Sawal: Kaan ki sab se chhoti haddi?
Jawab: Stapes - sirf ~2.8 mm (jism ki sab se chhoti haddi).
Sawal: Insaan ke kitne genes?
Jawab: ~20,000 (protein-coding).
Sawal: Chromosomes kitne?
Jawab: 46 (23 jorey).
Sawal: Jism me paani kitna percent?
Jawab: ~60% (baligh).
Sawal: Neurons ki raftar (signal)?
Jawab: Taqreeban 120 m/s tak.
Sawal: Zubaan par kitne taste kafla?
Jawab: 5 main - meetha, khatta, namkeen, karwa, umami.
Sawal: Baal mahine me kitne barhte hain?
Jawab: ~1.25 cm.
Sawal: Nakhoon mahine me kitne barhte hain?
Jawab: ~3.5 mm (hath).
Sawal: Aankh kitni dafa blink karti hai (min)?
Jawab: ~15-20 dafa.
Sawal: Dimagh jism ki energy kitni kharch karta hai?
Jawab: Taqreeban 20% (halke wazan ke-bawajood).
Sawal: Gurday (kidney) din me kitna khoon chhanta hai?
Jawab: Taqreeban 180 litre filter karta hai.
Sawal: Insulin kahan banta hai?
Jawab: Pancreas (lithar) me.
Sawal: Insaan ke daant kitne?
Jawab: Bara-ho-kar 32 (wisdom teeth included).
Sawal: Vaccine kaise kaam karti hai?
Jawab: Jism ki immune-memory banati hai - bimaari ke-bagher protection.
Sawal: Antibiotic bacteria par kaam karti hai ya virus par?
Jawab: Sirf bacteria par; virus par nahi.
Sawal: Photosynthesis kya hai?
Jawab: Paudon ka sooraj-ki-roshni + paani + CO2 se glucose + oxygen banana (chlorophyll se).
Sawal: Pauday raat me kya lete hain?
Jawab: Oxygen consume aur CO2 dete hain (din me ulta net).
Sawal: Machli ka dil kitne chamber?
Jawab: 2; magar Octopus ke 3 dil hote hain.
Sawal: Hathi ka dimagh kitna bhari?
Jawab: ~5 kg (zameen ke sab se bhare dimaghon me).
Sawal: Cheetah ki raftaar?
Jawab: ~100-120 km/h (short dash me).
Sawal: Falcon ki doobak (dive) raftaar?
Jawab: 300 km/h se zyada.
Sawal: Blue whale kitni badi?
Jawab: ~30 m lambi, 150+ ton - zameen ki sab se badi jaan-dar.
Sawal: Sab se badi machli?
Jawab: Whale Shark (~12 m).
Sawal: Sab se chhota parinda?
Jawab: Bee Hummingbird (Cuba) - ~5 cm.
Sawal: Ostrich ka anda kitna bhari?
Jawab: ~1.5 kg - sab se bada anda.
Sawal: Unt ka kubaa (hump) me kya hota hai?
Jawab: Charbi (fat) - paani nahi.
Sawal: Giraffe ki gardan me kitne vertebrae?
Jawab: 7 - insan jaisa hi.
Sawal: Kutte ki soonghne ki taqat?
Jawab: Insaan se 10,000-100,000 guna behtar.
Sawal: Billi (cat) ke kaan ke muscles?
Jawab: Taqreeban 32 - har taraf ghoomne ke liye.
Sawal: Dharpan (dolphin) neend kaise leta hai?
Jawab: Aadha dimagh ek-ek karke - unihemispheric sleep.
Sawal: Makhkhi (fly) ki aankhein?
Jawab: Compound - hazaaron chhote lens.
Sawal: Chinti me phephray hote hain?
Jawab: Nahi - wo aise hi saans lete hain (tracheal system).
Sawal: Machli paani me kaise saans leti hai?
Jawab: Gill (aab-parag) se oxygen nikaal-kar.
Sawal: Uss (camel) paani kitna peeta hai?
Jawab: 10 minute me 100+ litre.
Sawal: Sab se badi beti-jamai species (population)?
Jawab: Insaan, gay (cattle), bakri, murghi (chicken).
Sawal: Murghi (chicken) duniya me kitni hain?
Jawab: ~25-35 billion (sab se zyada parinda).
Sawal: Shehad-ki-makhi (bee) kya karti hai?
Jawab: Pollination + shehad; 1/3 crops pollination se depend.
Sawal: Reechh (bear) sardi me kya karta hai?
Jawab: Hibernation - ghaas-patti me so-kar energy bachata hai.
Sawal: Snake apni zubaan se kya karta hai?
Jawab: Smell (Jacobson organ) - zubaan hawa ke zarray laati hai.
Sawal: Chuha (rat) ki daerh?
Jawab: Umar ~1-2 saal; kunvaan (teeth) hamesha barhte hain.
Sawal: Haathi apni soond se kya karta hai?
Jawab: Soonghna, uthana, paani peena, awaaz - 40,000+ muscle units.
Sawal: Bander (monkey) ki poonch?
Jawab: Jungle-bander (Old World) me poonchh kaam nahi karta; New World bandar (spider monkey) poonchh se latkta hai.
Sawal: Kabootar ghar kaise dhoondta hai?
Jawab: Earth-magnetic field + sun + landmarks se navigation.
Sawal: Eagle ki aankh kitni tez?
Jawab: Insaan se 4-8 guna tez - bahut door se dekh leti hai.
Sawal: Machhli jald-zaya hote hain?
Jawab: Nahi - magar kuch species land par bhi thodi der sakti hain (mudskipper).
Sawal: Sab se zeher-ila machli?
Jawab: Stonefish (bahut zeher-ila dard).
Sawal: King Cobra kitni lambi?
Jawab: ~5.5 m tak - sab se lamba zeher-ila sanp.
Sawal: Python kitni lambi ho sakti hai?
Jawab: ~7 m tak (Reticulated python).
Sawal: Samandar ki sab se zeher-ila cheez?
Jawab: Box Jellyfish - dil-band karne wala zeher.
Sawal: Kaala-aswad (panda) ka khaana?
Jawab: Taqreeban 99% bamboo.
Sawal: Kangaaroo ki jeeb (pouch)?
Jawab: Bacche (jo incomplete paida hote hain) ko 6-9 mahine wahan rakha jata hai.
Sawal: Penguin kis meh me rahta hai?
Jawab: Antarctica aur Southern-hemisphere; Emperor penguin -60C bardasht karta hai.
Sawal: Title (tiger) ke patrol area kitne bare?
Jawab: ~20-1000 km^2 (khaana milne par depend).
Sawal: Sher (lion) ka raaj muheet?
Jawab: Pride - 10-15 bandon ki jama'at, zyada-tar auratein.
Sawal: Bhaloo (polar bear) ka rang?
Jawab: Khaal ke neeche kaala skin; baal hollow (white dikhta hai).
Sawal: Zebra ki dhaariyan kyun?
Jawab: Social-ID + machharon ko bhagane + shikan (camouflage) me madad.
Sawal: Rhino ka seengh?
Jawab: Keratin - nakhun jaisa hi material.
Sawal: Hiran (deer) ke seengh har saal?
Jawab: Girte aur naye ugte hain (antlers shed).
Sawal: Ullu (owl) raat me kaise dekhta hai?
Jawab: Bare aankh + extra rod-cells; face ka dish sound focus karta hai.
Sawal: Kabootar ki umar?
Jawab: ~3-5 saal wild; 15+ pet me.
Sawal: Dhadkan (pulse) ka normal?
Jawab: 60-100 beats/minute (aarum se).
Sawal: Din me kitna paani peena chahiye?
Jawab: ~2-3 litre (mausam/mehnat par depend).
Sawal: Neend ki miqdar baligh ke liye?
Jawab: 7-9 ghante.
Sawal: Insaan ke body-cells kitne?
Jawab: ~30-37 trillion.
Sawal: Kapalon (hair) ki umar?
Jawab: Har baal 2-6 saal ka cycle karta hai.
Sawal: Khansi (cough) ki raftaar?
Jawab: ~90 km/h tak hawa nikalti hai.
Sawal: Cheenk (sneeze) ki raftaar?
Jawab: 150-160 km/h tak.
Sawal: Sarr (head) par baal kitne?
Jawab: Taqreeban 100,000 (blonde ~150,000, red ~90,000).
Sawal: Baal ki raftaar (growth) me farq?
Jawab: Saari jagah par behtareen summer me barhta hai (hormones aur mausam se).
Sawal: Naakhun sab se tez kaun sa barhta hai?
Jawab: Hath ki beech-wali ungali ka naakhun.
Sawal: Chai (tea) ki tarikh?
Jawab: China me ~2700 BC se (pauranik riwayat).
Sawal: Coffee kis mulk me sab se zyada ugti hai?
Jawab: Brazil.
Sawal: Cheeni (sugar) kis se banti hai?
Jawab: Sugar-cane (~80%) aur sugar-beet (~20%).
Sawal: Makka (Makkah) ki location?
Jawab: Saudi Arabia - Masjid-ul-Haram wahan hai.
Sawal: Masjid-ul-Haram me kitne log namaz parh sakte hain?
Jawab: ~2.5 million (expansion ke sath).
Sawal: Kaba (Kaaba) ki bulandi?
Jawab: ~13-15 m (cube-shape); Black Stone (Hajr-e-Aswad) south-east corner me.
Sawal: Safa aur Marwa kya hain?
Jawab: Sa'ee ke do pahar - Hajj/Umrah ke rukn.
Sawal: Zam-zam ka paani kahan hai?
Jawab: Makkah - Masjid-ul-Haram ke qareeb; 4000+ saal purana chashma.
Sawal: Madina ki purani masjid?
Jawab: Masjid-e-Quba - Islam ki pehli masjid (Prophet ke waqt).
Sawal: Arafat ka maidan kahan hai?
Jawab: Makkah se ~20 km - Hajj ka rukn (9 Dhul-Hijjah).
Sawal: Mina kya hai?
Jawab: Hajj ka khema-elaqa (Jamarat ki rammi yahan hoti hai).
Sawal: Uhud ka pahar kahan hai?
Jawab: Madina ke shumal - Ghazwa-e-Uhud (3 AH) ki jagah.
Sawal: Badr kahan hai?
Jawab: Madina se ~130 km shumal-maghrib - Ghazwa-e-Badr (2 AH).
Sawal: Masjid-e-Nabawi ki sab se pehli shakal?
Jawab: Prophet  (SAW) ne Madina me khajoor-ke-tanne se banai (622 AD).
Sawal: Azaan kab shuru hui?
Jawab: 1 AH (Madina) - Hazrat Bilal RA pehle muezzin.
Sawal: Qibla ka tabdeeli kab hui?
Jawab: 2 AH - Bait-ul-Maqdis se Makkah (Kaaba) ki taraf.
Sawal: Hajj ka mahina?
Jawab: Dhul-Hijjah (Islamic calendar ka 12th mahina).
Sawal: Ramadan kaunsa mahina hai?
Jawab: Islamic calendar ka 9th - roza (fasting) ka mahina.
Sawal: Zakat ki sharah?
Jawab: 2.5% (saal-guzar maal par, nisab puhanchne par).
Sawal: Umrah ke rukn kitne?
Jawab: Ihram, Tawaf, Sa'ee, Halaq/Taqsir.
Sawal: Hajj ke rukn kitne?
Jawab: Ihram, Arafah wuqoof, Tawaf-uz-Ziyarah, Sa'ee (aur rammi/tahallul mashaq-hal).
Sawal: Laylatul-Qadr kya hai?
Jawab: Ramadan ke aakhri das raaton me odd (taqreeban 27th) - Quran ka naazul.
Sawal: Eid-ul-Fitr kab?
Jawab: 1 Shawwal - Ramadan ke baad.
Sawal: Eid-ul-Adha kab?
Jawab: 10 Dhul-Hijjah - Hajj ke din.
Sawal: Ashura kaunsa din?
Jawab: 10 Muharram.
Sawal: Namaz farz kitni?
Jawab: 5 waqt - Fajr, Zuhr, Asr, Maghrib, Isha.
Sawal: Fajr ke farz rakat?
Jawab: 2.
Sawal: Zuhr ke farz rakat?
Jawab: 4.
Sawal: Asr ke farz rakat?
Jawab: 4.
Sawal: Maghrib ke farz rakat?
Jawab: 3.
Sawal: Isha ke farz rakat?
Jawab: 4 (+ witr 3 wajib-haq).
Sawal: Witr kitne rakat?
Jawab: 3 (Hanafi me wajib).
Sawal: Friday prayer ka naam?
Jawab: Jumu'ah - 2 rakat fard + khutbah.
Sawal: Taraweeh kab parhi jati hai?
Jawab: Ramadan me Isha ke baad.
Sawal: Wuzu ke faraiz kitne?
Jawab: 4 (Quran 5:6) - chehra, hath kohni tak, sar ka masah, paon takhnoo tak.
Sawal: Ghusl ke faraiz?
Jawab: 3 - muh (kull-muh), naak (kull-naak), poori jism.
Sawal: Tayammum kab?
Jawab: Jab paani na mile ya istemal mumkin na ho.
Sawal: Quran me kitni surahs?
Jawab: 114.
Sawal: Quran me kitni aayaat?
Jawab: Taqreeban 6,236 (riwayat-kahfi par farq).
Sawal: Quran ke kitne juz (paray)?
Jawab: 30.
Sawal: Sab se lambi surah?
Jawab: Surah Al-Baqarah - 286 aayaat.
Sawal: Sab se chhoti surah?
Jawab: Surah Al-Kausar - 3 aayaat.
Sawal: Pehli wahi kab aayi?
Jawab: 610 AD - Cave Hira, Surah Al-Alaq ke pehle 5 aayaat ("Iqra").
Sawal: Wahi kitne saal me mukammal?
Jawab: Taqreeban 23 saal.
Sawal: Quran ki compilation kiski zabtagi?
Jawab: Hazrat Uthman RA ke daur me ik mushaf (single-script) banaya gaya.
Sawal: Quran ka pehla lafz?
Jawab: "Iqra" (parho).
Sawal: Fatiha kitni aayaat?
Jawab: 7 - Quran ki pehli surah.
Sawal: Ayat-ul-Kursi kis surah me?
Jawab: Surah Al-Baqarah, aayat 255.
Sawal: Kalima Tayyibah kya hai?
Jawab: "La ilaha illa-Allah, Muhammad-ur-Rasulullah" - Islam ka pehla kalima.
Sawal: Islam ke 5 rukn?
Jawab: Shahadah, Namaz, Roza, Zakat, Hajj.
Sawal: Iman ke 6 arkaan?
Jawab: Allah, Farishtay, Kitabein, Rasool, Aakhirat, Taqdeer.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ki wafat?
Jawab: 632 AD (11 AH), Madina.
Sawal: Hijrah kab hui?
Jawab: 622 AD - Makkah se Madina (Islamic calendar yahan se shuru).
Sawal: Ghazwa-e-Badr kab?
Jawab: 2 AH (624 AD) - 313 Muslims vs ~1000 mushrikeen.
Sawal: Ghazwa-e-Khandaq kab?
Jawab: 5 AH (627 AD) - trench (khandaq) ki hifazat.
Sawal: Fatah-e-Makkah kab?
Jawab: 8 AH (630 AD) - baghair khoon-kharabe.
Sawal: Hajjatul-Wida kab?
Jawab: 10 AH (632 AD) - Prophet  (SAW) ka aakhri Hajj.
Sawal: Khulafa-e-Rashideen kitne?
Jawab: 4 - Abu Bakr, Umar, Uthman, Ali (RA).
Sawal: Hazrat Abu Bakr RA ka khilafat?
Jawab: 632-634 AD (2 saal).
Sawal: Hazrat Umar RA ka khilafat?
Jawab: 634-644 AD (10 saal).
Sawal: Hazrat Uthman RA ka khilafat?
Jawab: 644-656 AD (12 saal).
Sawal: Hazrat Ali RA ka khilafat?
Jawab: 656-661 AD (4-5 saal).
Sawal: Umayyad khilafat ka markaz?
Jawab: Damascus (661-750 AD).
Sawal: Abbasid khilafat ka markaz?
Jawab: Baghdad (750-1258 AD).
Sawal: Constantinople kab fatah hua?
Jawab: 1453 - Sultan Mehmed II (Ottoman).
Sawal: Ottoman khilafat kitne saal?
Jawab: Taqreeban 1299-1922 (~623 saal).
Sawal: Muhammad bin Qasim Sindh kab aaya?
Jawab: 712 AD.
Sawal: Babur ka Panipat?
Jawab: 1526 - Mughal saltanat ki bunyaad.
Sawal: Taj Mahal kisne banaya?
Jawab: Shah Jahan (1632-1653) - Mumtaz Mahal ke maqbaray ke liye.
Sawal: Al-Aqsa kahan hai?
Jawab: Jerusalem - Islam ki pehli qibla, teesra muqaddas masjid.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ki pehli biwi?
Jawab: Hazrat Khadijah RA.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ki betiyan?
Jawab: Hazrat Zainab, Ruqayyah, Umm Kulthum, Fatimah (RA).
Sawal: Hazrat Fatimah RA ki betiyan?
Jawab: Hazrat Zainab aur Umm Kulthum (RA).
Sawal: Imam Bukhari ka asal naam?
Jawab: Muhammad ibn Ismail al-Bukhari (810-870 AD).
Sawal: Sahih Bukhari me kitne ahadith?
Jawab: ~7,563 (riwayat ke hisaab se; dohra mil-kar ~2,602 unique).
Sawal: Sahih Muslim ka author?
Jawab: Muslim ibn al-Hajjaj (815-875 AD).
Sawal: Six Sahih Sitta kaun si books?
Jawab: Bukhari, Muslim, Abu Dawud, Tirmidhi, Nasai, Ibn Majah.
Sawal: Muwatta Malik kis ke?
Jawab: Imam Malik ibn Anas (~795 AD).
Sawal: Fiqh ke 4 mazhab?
Jawab: Hanafi, Shafi'i, Maliki, Hanbali (sab Sunni; ikhtilaf fiqhi hai).
Sawal: Hadith kya hai?
Jawab: Prophet  (SAW) ke alfaaz, amal aur tasdeeqat ki sunni-recorded riwayat.
Sawal: Sunnah kya hai?
Jawab: Prophet  (SAW) ka tareeqa (amal) jo ummat ke liye hukm-nama hai.
Sawal: Sahaba kaun hain?
Jawab: Prophet  (SAW) ke saath rahne wale musalman (khalifa-ke-baad-pasand iman-lane wale).
Sawal: Ashra Mubashra kaun hain?
Jawab: 10 Sahaba jinhe duniya me jannat ki basharat mili.
Sawal: Muhajireen kaun?
Jawab: Makkah se Madina hijrat karne wale Sahaba.
Sawal: Ansar kaun?
Jawab: Madina ke musalman jo Muhajireen ko ghar-paani dete the.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ki tareekh-e-pedaish?
Jawab: 571 AD (Year of the Elephant), Makkah.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ka pita?
Jawab: Abdullah; maa: Aminah - dono ka inteqal Prophet bachpan me hua.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ke dada?
Jawab: Abd al-Muttalib - jinhome Prophet  (SAW) ke parwarish wale.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ki ghulam-azad?
Jawab: Zayd ibn Harithah RA - Prophet ke maula; sirf usi ka naam Quran me likha hai (Surah 33:37).
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ka kaam (shuru)?
Jawab: Tijarat (bazargani) - Khadijah RA ke karwan me.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ka laqab (pre-nubuwwat)?
Jawab: Al-Amin (Sadiq-ul-Ameen).
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ki hijrat ki waqat ka sathi?
Jawab: Hazrat Abu Bakr RA (Ghar-e-Thawr me 3 din).
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ke mashhoor uncle?
Jawab: Abu Talib (parwarish-karne wale), Hamza RA aur Abbas RA (sahaba), Abu Lahab (gair-Muslim).
Sawal: Ghazwa-e-Tabuk kab?
Jawab: 9 AH - aakhri bada ghazwa (gharmi ke safar me).
Sawal: Bai'at-e-Ridwan kab?
Jawab: 6 AH - Hudaibiyah ke baad ka mashaq-hal bai'at.
Sawal: Hudaibiyah ka sulah?
Jawab: 6 AH - 10-saal ka sulah (magar 2 saal me Makkah-walo ne toda).
Sawal: Makkah ki fatah ke baad kya hua?
Jawab: Aam maafi (general amnesty) - sirf kuch logon ko (jinke qanooni jurm) saza mili.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ka aakhri khutbah kahan?
Jawab: Arafat (Hajjatul-Wida) - insan-e-azad ki barabari par.
Sawal: Makkah ka purani naam?
Jawab: Bakkah (Quran me), ma'na "jama'a karne wali".
Sawal: Madina ka purana naam?
Jawab: Yathrib; "Madinat-un-Nabi" (Prophet ka shehar).
Sawal: Miraj kab hui?
Jawab: Isra-Miraj - mashhoor bayaan 27 Rajab; riwayat me tarikh par ikhtilaf hai.
Sawal: Miraj me kya hua?
Jawab: Prophet  (SAW) Makkah se Bait-ul-Maqdis, phir 7 aasmanon tak - 50 namaz ko 5 par baraabari me tabdeel.
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ka asal usool?
Jawab: Akhlaq - "Main magar akhlaq kamali ke liye bheja gaya hoon" (mashhoor riwayat).
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ke aakhri lafz?
Jawab: "As-salah, as-salah" (namaz ki hifazat ki wasiyat).
Sawal: Prophet  (SAW) ki qabar kahan?
Jawab: Masjid-e-Nabawi, Madina (Hujrah-e-Aisha RA ke-andar).
Sawal: Hadith ke darjat?
Jawab: Main teen: Sahih (sab se pakka), Hasan (achha), Da'if (kamzor) - isnad aur matn ki tasdeeq par.
Sawal: Hadith-e-Qudsi kya hai?
Jawab: Prophet  (SAW) ki riwayat me Allah ke alfaaz (matn qudsi, isnad nabi).
Sawal: Quran ki sab se lambi aayat?
Jawab: Aayat-ud-Dayn (Al-Baqarah 282); sab se buzurg aayat: Ayat-ul-Kursi (Al-Baqarah 255).
Sawal: Pi (pi) ki qeemat?
Jawab: Taqreeban 3.14159 - circle ke qutr (diameter) aur circumferer ka nisba.
Sawal: e (Euler's number)?
Jawab: Taqreeban 2.71828.
Sawal: Prime number kya hai?
Jawab: Wo number jo sirf 1 aur apne-aap se divide ho (2, 3, 5, 7, 11...).
Sawal: Sab se chhota prime number?
Jawab: 2 - aur ye akela even prime bhi hai.
Sawal: 100 se pehle kitne primes?
Jawab: 25.
Sawal: Perfect number kya hai?
Jawab: Wo number jiske proper-divisors ka sum khud ho (6 = 1+2+3; 28 bhi).
Sawal: Fibonacci sequence kya hai?
Jawab: Har number pichle do ka sum - 0, 1, 1, 2, 3, 5, 8, 13, 21...
Sawal: Golden ratio?
Jawab: Taqreeban 1.618 (phi ) - Fibonacci ratios ka limit.
Sawal: Pythagoras theorem?
Jawab: Right-triangle me a^2 + b^2 = c^2 (hypotenuse c).
Sawal: Triangle ke angles ka sum?
Jawab: 180.
Sawal: Circle me kitne degrees?
Jawab: 360.
Sawal: 1 degree me kitne minutes?
Jawab: 60 arc-minutes.
Sawal: Area of circle?
Jawab: pi  x  r^2.
Sawal: Circumference of circle?
Jawab: 2pi  x  r (ya pi  x  d).
Sawal: Area of triangle?
Jawab:   x  base  x  height.
Sawal: Volume of sphere?
Jawab: (4/3)pi  x  r^3.
Sawal: Volume of cylinder?
Jawab: pi  x  r^2  x  h.
Sawal: Mashhoor Pythagorean triple?
Jawab: 3-4-5 (3^2 + 4^2 = 5^2).
Sawal: Factorial kya hai?
Jawab: n! = 1 x 2 x ... x n; e.g., 5! = 120.
Sawal: Kisi number ki power 0?
Jawab: 1 (nonzero number ke liye).
Sawal: Do negative numbers ki zarb?
Jawab: Positive (minus  x  minus = plus).
Sawal: PEMDAS/BODMAS kya hai?
Jawab: Calculation ka tarteeb: Brackets, Orders, Divide/Multiply, Add/Subtract.
Sawal: 0.999... aur 1 me farq?
Jawab: Nahi - 0.9999... barabar 1 hai (mathematically).
Sawal: 1 prime hai?
Jawab: Nahi - prime ke liye 2 divisors chahiye; 1 ka sirf 1.
Sawal: Average (mean) kaise?
Jawab: Sab numbers ka sum  /  count.
Sawal: Median kya hai?
Jawab: Sort karke beech-wala number.
Sawal: Mode kya hai?
Jawab: Sab se zyada baar aane wala number.
Sawal: Percentage kaise nikale?
Jawab: (Part  /  Whole)  x  100.
Sawal: Simple interest formula?
Jawab: P  x  R  x  T  /  100.
Sawal: Compound interest kya hai?
Jawab: Interest par interest - A = P(1 + r).
Sawal: LCM kya hai?
Jawab: Least Common Multiple - do numbers ka sab se chhota common multiple.
Sawal: HCF/GCD kya hai?
Jawab: Highest Common Factor - sab se bara common divisor.
Sawal: Binary system kya hai?
Jawab: Base-2 - sirf 0 aur 1 (computers ki zubaan).
Sawal: Hexadecimal?
Jawab: Base-16 - 0-9 aur A-F.
Sawal: Roman numerals me 10?
Jawab: X (I=1, V=5, X=10, L=50, C=100, D=500, M=1000).
Sawal: 1 kilometer me kitne meter?
Jawab: 1,000.
Sawal: 1 meter me kitne centimeter?
Jawab: 100.
Sawal: 1 kilogram me kitne gram?
Jawab: 1,000.
Sawal: 1 tonne me kitne kilogram?
Jawab: 1,000.
Sawal: 1 inch me kitne centimeter?
Jawab: 2.54.
Sawal: 1 mile me kitne kilometer?
Jawab: Taqreeban 1.609.
Sawal: 1 gallon (US) me kitne litre?
Jawab: Taqreeban 3.785.
Sawal: 1 pound me kitne kilogram?
Jawab: Taqreeban 0.454.
Sawal: Pentagon me kitne sides?
Jawab: 5 (hexagon 6, heptagon 7, octagon 8, nonagon 9, decagon 10).
Sawal: Googol kya hai?
Jawab: 1 ke sath 100 zeros (10).
Sawal: Dozen kya hai?
Jawab: 12; Gross = 144.
Sawal: 1 billion me kitne zeros (short scale)?
Jawab: 9 (10); trillion = 12 zeros.
Sawal: Sab se pehla calculator jaisa tool?
Jawab: Abacus (Babylon/China) - hazaaron saal purana.
Sawal: Zero (0) ka invention?
Jawab: Indian mathematicians ne zero ko number banaya; Brahmagupta ne rules likhe (~628 AD).
Sawal: Algebra ka asal?
Jawab: "Al-jabr" - al-Khwarizmi ki book se (9th century, Baghdad).
Sawal: Geometry ki kitab?
Jawab: Euclid's Elements (~300 BC) - 13 books.
Sawal: 180 aur pi ka relation?
Jawab: pi radians = 180.
Sawal: Number line par negative left?
Jawab: Haan - 0 se left negative, right positive.
Sawal: Evergreen math shortcut (11  x  do-digit)?
Jawab: 11  x  35 = 385 (digits ko 3_5 me sum beech me).
Sawal: 5 se zarb ka shortcut?
Jawab:  x 5 =  x 10  /  2.
Sawal: 9 ki zarb ka pattern?
Jawab: 9, 18, 27, 36... digits ka sum hamesha 9 (9 ki table me).
Sawal: 25% of 80?
Jawab: 20 (25% =  / 4).
Sawal: 10% of 350?
Jawab: 35 (decimal-point left me 1).
Sawal: Everest kahan hai?
Jawab: Nepal-China border - Himalaya.
Sawal: K2 kahan hai?
Jawab: Pakistan-China border - Karakoram.
Sawal: Nile kahan behta hai?
Jawab: Africa - Egypt se Mediterranean me.
Sawal: Amazon kahan hai?
Jawab: South America - Atlantic me girta hai.
Sawal: Indus (Sindh) darya kahan se nikalta hai?
Jawab: Tibet (China) - Ladakh-KPK-Punjab-Sindh se Arab Sea.
Sawal: Jhelum, Chenab, Ravi, Sutlej?
Jawab: Punjab ke 5 darya - "Punjab" = panj (5) + aab (paani).
Sawal: Dead Sea kahan hai?
Jawab: Jordan-Israel border - itna namkeen ke machli nahi rehti.
Sawal: Sahara kahan hai?
Jawab: North Africa - 11+ mulkon me.
Sawal: Thar desert kahan hai?
Jawab: Sindh (Pakistan) aur India border.

Jawab: Chile/Argentina (Cape Horn ke sath).
Sawal: Australia ka mashhoor patthar?
Jawab: Uluru (Ayers Rock).

Jawab: Arizona, USA - 446 km lamba, ~1.8 km gehra.
Sawal: Kilimanjaro kahan hai?
Jawab: Tanzania - Africa ka sab se ooncha pahar (5,895 m).
Sawal: Mount Fuji kahan hai?
Jawab: Japan - 3,776 m (volcano).
Sawal: Alps kahan hain?
Jawab: Europe - France/Switzerland/Italy/Austria.
Sawal: Rocky Mountains?
Jawab: North America (Canada se USA).
Sawal: Ural Mountains kya alag karte hain?
Jawab: Europe aur Asia (Russia).
Sawal: Andes kaunse mulkon me?
Jawab: Venezuela se Chile tak (7 mulk).
Sawal: Bosphorus kahan hai?
Jawab: Istanbul (Turkey) - Europe-Asia ka samandari rasta.
Sawal: Strait of Gibraltar?
Jawab: Spain-Morocco ke darmiyan - Mediterranean ka darwaza.
Sawal: Bermuda Triangle kahan hai?
Jawab: Miami-Bermuda-Puerto Rico triangle (myth + mahir tafseer: natural).
Sawal: International Date Line kahan hai?
Jawab: Pacific me ~180 longitude par - tarikh yahan badalti hai.
Sawal: Tropic of Cancer?
Jawab: 23.5 North latitude.
Sawal: Tropic of Capricorn?
Jawab: 23.5 South latitude.
Sawal: Arctic Circle?
Jawab: 66.5 North.
Sawal: Sahel kya hai?
Jawab: Sahara ke dakshin ka belt (Africa) - registan aur savanna ke darmiyan.

Jawab: Tanzania - wildebeest migration mashhoor.

Jawab: Peru (Andes) - Inca civilization (~1450 AD).

Jawab: Jordan - Nabatean sheher (pathar-me cut).

Jawab: Taqreeban 21,196 km (sab branches).
Sawal: Statue of Liberty kahan hai?
Jawab: New York (France ne 1886 me gift ki).

Jawab: Taqreeban 330 m - Paris (1889).

Jawab: 828 m - Dubai (duniya ka sab se ooncha building).

Jawab: Rome, Italy (~80 AD).
Sawal: Chichen Itza kahan hai?
Jawab: Mexico - Maya civilization.
Sawal: Christ the Redeemer kahan hai?
Jawab: Rio de Janeiro, Brazil.

Jawab: Agra, India - Yamuna darya ke kinare.

Jawab: Islamabad - Turkey-ke-architect Vedat Dalokay ka design.

Jawab: Lahore - Aurangzeb (1673).
Sawal: Lahore ka shalimar bagh kisne banaya?

Jawab: Thatta, Sindh - duniya ke bare maqbarey (UNESCO).
Sawal: Hingol National Park?
Jawab: Balochistan - Pakistan ka bara park.
Sawal: Attabad Lake kahan hai?
Jawab: Hunza, GB - 2010 landslide se bani jheel.
Sawal: Pakistan me kitne UNESCO sites?
Jawab: 6 - Mohenjo-daro, Taxila, Takht-i-Bahi, Thatta (Makli), Lahore Fort+Shalimar, Rohtas Fort.
Sawal: Computer ka dimagh kaunsa part?
Jawab: CPU (Central Processing Unit).
Sawal: RAM kya hai?
Jawab: Random Access Memory - temporary (volatile) memory, band hone par jaata hai.
Sawal: HDD vs SSD?
Jawab: HDD magnet-disc (dheema, sasta); SSD flash-chips (tez, mehnga).
Sawal: 1 byte me kitne bits?
Jawab: 8.
Sawal: 1 KB me kitne bytes?
Jawab: 1,024 (binary me) - marketing me 1,000.
Sawal: Transistor ka izhar (invention)?
Jawab: 1947 - Bell Labs (Bardeen, Brattain, Shockley).
Sawal: Integrated circuit kab?
Jawab: 1958 - Jack Kilby (Texas Instruments).
Sawal: Moore's Law kya hai?
Jawab: Har ~2 saal me chip par transistors double (trend, ab dheema).
Sawal: ENIAC kya thi?
Jawab: 1945-46 ki pehli badi electronic computer (USA).
Sawal: Pehla programmer kaun?
Jawab: Ada Lovelace (1840s) - Babbage ke Analytical Engine ke notes.
Sawal: Charles Babbage kis ki bunyaad?
Jawab: Analytical Engine - modern computer ka pahlwan-concept.
Sawal: Alan Turing kis ke abba?
Jawab: Computer-Science ka "abba" - Turing machine concept (1936).
Sawal: Internet ki bunyaad?
Jawab: ARPANET (1969, USA) - 4 universities.
Sawal: WWW kisne banaya?
Jawab: Tim Berners-Lee - 1989 (CERN).
Sawal: Email kab?
Jawab: 1971 - Ray Tomlinson (pehli dafa @ symbol).

Jawab: 1998 - Larry Page aur Sergey Brin.

Jawab: 2004 - Mark Zuckerberg (Harvard).

Jawab: 2005.

Jawab: 2001.

Jawab: James Gosling - 1995 (Sun Microsystems).
Sawal: C language kisne?
Jawab: Dennis Ritchie - 1972 (Bell Labs).
Sawal: C++ kisne?
Jawab: Bjarne Stroustrup - 1985.
Sawal: JavaScript kisne?
Jawab: Brendan Eich - 1995 (10 din me pehla version).
Sawal: Linux kisne?
Jawab: Linus Torvalds - 1991 (open-source, free).
Sawal: Windows pehla version kab?
Jawab: 1985 (Microsoft 1975 me bani - Gates aur Allen).
Sawal: Apple kab bani?
Jawab: 1976 - Steve Jobs, Steve Wozniak, Ronald Wayne.
Sawal: Pehla computer bug?
Jawab: 1947 - Harvard Mark II me asal pattay (moth) mila.
Sawal: GitHub kab?
Jawab: 2008 - code-hosting (Microsoft ne 2018 me khareeda).
Sawal: API kya hai?
Jawab: Application Programming Interface - do softwares ka baat-cheet ka rasta.
Sawal: Database SQL kya hai?
Jawab: Structured data ki table-bhasha (rows/columns).
Sawal: HTML kya hai?
Jawab: HyperText Markup Language - web-page ka dhaancha.
Sawal: CSS kya hai?
Jawab: Cascading Style Sheets - web-page ki rang-shakal.
Sawal: HTTP kya hai?
Jawab: HyperText Transfer Protocol - browser-server ki guftagu.
Sawal: Wi-Fi kab?
Jawab: 1997 (IEEE 802.11 standard).
Sawal: Bluetooth kab?
Jawab: 1994 - Ericsson (Danish king "Blatand" ke naam par).
Sawal: GPS kitne satellites?
Jawab: ~24+ active - zameen ke 4 se zyada chahiye fix ke liye.
Sawal: Pixel kya hai?
Jawab: Picture element - screen ki sab se chhoti unit.
Sawal: 1920 x 1080 kya hai?
Jawab: Full HD resolution - 2+ million pixels.
Sawal: GPU kis ke liye?
Jawab: Graphics Processing Unit - images/game/AI-calculation.
Sawal: CPU GHz kya batata hai?
Jawab: Har second ke cycles (billions).
Sawal: Machine learning kya hai?
Jawab: Data se rules khud seekhne wala software (deep-learning subset hai).
Sawal: Neural network kya hai?
Jawab: Brain-inspired layers - input se output tabdeeli ke weights.
Sawal: Algorithm kya hai?
Jawab: Masla hal karne ke qadam-ke-qadam usool (recipe jaisa).
Sawal: Blockchain kya hai?
Jawab: Aisi data-chain jo sab nodes par ek-jaisi hai (tamper-harcana).
Sawal: Bitcoin kab?
Jawab: 2009 - Satoshi Nakamoto (pseudonym; asli shakhs mashhoor nahi).
Sawal: Encryption kya hai?
Jawab: Data ko sirf-key-wale ke liye parhney-ke-layaq banana (RSA 1977).
Sawal: Open-source kya hai?
Jawab: Aisi software jiska code public hai - koi bhi parh/badal sakta hai.
Sawal: AI ka full form?
Jawab: Artificial Intelligence - machina-se-insani-hunar ki naqal.
Sawal: 1 GB me kitne MB?
Jawab: 1,024 (binary) / 1,000 (marketing).
Sawal: 1 TB me kitne GB?
Jawab: Taqreeban 1,024.
Sawal: Antivirus kya karta hai?
Jawab: Malware (virus) ko dhoond-kar block karta hai.
Sawal: Password ka behtareen usool?
Jawab: Lamba + unique (har site ke liye alag) + 2FA.

Jawab: Fake-email/site se password/maal jhoolna.
Sawal: USB ka full form?
Jawab: Universal Serial Bus (1996).
Sawal: Mouse ka izhar?
Jawab: Douglas Engelbart - 1964.

Jawab: 1870s typewriter se - jam-se-bachne ke liye layout (myth + asal: telegraph-era).

Jawab: 1960s concept; 2007 iPhone ne mass-market banaya.

Jawab: IBM Simon (1994) - touchscreen + apps.
Sawal: SIM card kya hai?
Jawab: Subscriber Identity Module - network ki pehchan.
Sawal: 5G kya hai?
Jawab: 5th-generation mobile network - tez + kam-latency.
Sawal: Cloud computing kya hai?
Jawab: Internet-par-hosted servers (apne PC par nahi).
Sawal: 404 error ka ma'ni?
Jawab: Page not found (HTTP status code).

Jawab: OK - request kaamyaab.
Sawal: Ping kya batata hai?
Jawab: Network latency (ms me jawab ka waqt).
Sawal: Firewall kya hai?
Jawab: Network ka darwaza-ban - gair-mamuli traffic roke.
Sawal: Cache kya hai?
Jawab: Tez-access ke liye temporary copy.
Sawal: Binary 101 kya hai?
Jawab: 5 (4+0+1).

Jawab: 15.

Jawab: 4 bits (half byte).

Jawab: Text-characters ke number-codes (A=65, a=97).
